- **pressure-test**: Stress-test substantive work — surface strengths, weaknesses, hidden assumptions, failure modes, and adjacent ideas, then return prioritized, actionable fixes (each weakness plus how to fix it). - **pressure-test**: Pressure-test a business idea in the order that kills bad ideas fastest. Use when assessing whether a business, product or side project is worth pursuing, or when a strategy needs stress-testing before commitment. - **pressure-test**: Adversarial plan review loop that stress-tests an implementation plan until it converges on behavioral exit criteria. Invoke whenever the user wants to: pressure-test, stress-test, red-team, or "grill" a plan/design/architecture; validate a plan before dispatching implementation; or whenever the PM skill marks a task `risk: high`. Also invoke when the user asks for "confidence" in a plan — this skill REPLACES numeric confidence claims with verifiable convergence criteria. Runs the architect (author) against the red-team-critic (adversary) in alternating passes. ([WayneBanksy/wayneys_claude](https://github.com/WayneBanksy/wayneys_claude/tree/HEAD/skills/pressure-test))

"Pressure-test this." / "We need a dashboard." / "Should we build this?" / "Customers keep asking for X." / "Spar on this bet."
